The pairing previously met at [[2024 in ONE Championship#ONE Friday Fights 85|ONE Friday Fights 85]] in November 2024, which Jarvis won by knockout in third round.\n\nAt the weigh-ins, four fighters failed to hydration test and missed weight for their respective fights:{{Cite web |date=2026-06-26 |title=\u0e1c\u0e25\u0e0a\u0e31\u0e48\u0e07\u0e19\u0e49\u0e33\u0e2b\u0e19\u0e31\u0e01\u0e41\u0e25\u0e30\u0e27\u0e31\u0e14\u0e23\u0e30\u0e14\u0e31\u0e1a\u0e19\u0e49\u0e33 \u0e28\u0e36\u0e01 ONE Fight Night 44: \u0e08\u0e2d\u0e23\u0e4c\u0e08 vs. \u0e23\u0e38\u0e48\u0e07\u0e23\u0e32\u0e27\u0e35 2|url=https://www.onefc.com/th/news/weight-and-hydration-results-of-one-fight-night-44/ |access-date=2026-06-26 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=th-TH}}\n*[[George Jarvis (Muay Thai)|George Jarvis]] weighted in at 171.2 pounds, 1.2 pounds over the lightweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to [[Rungrawee Sitsongpeenong]].\n*Johan Estupi\u00f1an weighted in at 139.2 pounds, 4.2 pounds over the flyweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to Akif Guluzada.\n*Charlie Guest weighted in at 160.2 pounds, 5.2 pounds over the featherweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to Samuel Fitzgerald.\n*Owen Jones weighted in at 158.6 pounds, 3.6 pounds over the featherweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to Fabricio Andrey.\n\n\n===Results===\n{| fighters failed to hydration test and missed weight for their respective fights:{{Cite web |date=2026-07-17 |title=\u0e1c\u0e25\u0e0a\u0e31\u0e48\u0e07\u0e19\u0e49\u0e33\u0e2b\u0e19\u0e31\u0e01\u0e41\u0e25\u0e30\u0e27\u0e31\u0e14\u0e23\u0e30\u0e14\u0e31\u0e1a\u0e19\u0e49\u0e33 \u0e28\u0e36\u0e01 ONE Fight |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=th-TH}}\n*Antonio Cesar weighted in at 141.4 pounds, 6.4 pounds over the flyweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to Lito Adiwang.\n*Felipe Lobo weighted in at 145.4 pounds, 0.4 pounds over the bantamweight limit and he was fined 30 percent of his purse which went to Dmitrii Kovtun.\n*Sean Climaco Therefore, a Muay Thai bout between Khunponnoi Sor.Sommai and Sunvo Torfunfarm headlined to event.\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the The pairing previously met at [[2025 in ONE Championship#ONE Friday Fights 120|ONE Friday Fights 120]] in August 2025, which Yodlekpet won by split decision. The second time they met at [[2025 in ONE Championship#ONE Friday Fights 126|ONE Friday Fights 126]] in September 2025, which Pompet won by split decision.\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Muangthai P.K.Saenchai'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! and YodUdon K.Tongtalingchan headlined the event.\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Khunsuk T1.FightAcademy, Sitthichai
